About Us
關於我們



As a next-generation stock investing assistant, we provide             作為新一代股票投資助手，我們面向廣大個人
equity investment tools, securities investment advisory, investor      投資者提供股票投資工具、證券投資顧問、投
education and other services for individual investors with             資者教育等服務，相繼推出股票行情軟件、股
product offerings such as stock quote software, Stock Learning         票學習機、股道領航系列、超級投資家、九爻
Machine, Stock Navigator Series , Super Investor and Jiuyao            股（即小額系列產品）等產品。
Stocks (our small-amount series products).

Implementing our “technology + investment research”                    我們秉持「科技＋投研」雙輪驅動戰略，基於
dual-driver strategy, we have developed JF Robo-Advisor,               人工智能與大數據技術研發九方智能投顧數字
FinSphere Agent, FinSphere Report and other AI products                人、九方靈犀、九方智研等AI產品，實現行業
based on our AI and big data technologies, enabling us to              領先的創新實踐與場景應用，致力於成為客戶
achieve industry leadership in innovative practice and scenario        投資理財的終身伴侶。
application, and we are striving to become a lifelong partner in
investment and wealth management for our customers.

professional, and enhance the sense of happiness in investment         幸福感
and wealth management


VISION                                                                 願景

management

Chairman’s Statement
主席報告



Dear Shareholders:                                                      尊敬的各位股東：

In 2024, China withstood economic challenges amid complex               2024年，中國經濟經受住複雜環境考驗，資
environment and experienced structural reform in the capital            本市場經歷結構性變革。在「穩中求進、以
market. Under the policy guidance of “pursuing progress while           進促穩」政策指引下，A股呈現「V」型反轉。
maintaining stability, and promoting stability through progress”,       政策環境持續優化與經結構轉型升級築牢資
the A-share market staged a V-shaped upturn. Continuous policy          本市場堅實根基，使其於波動中展現強勁韌
refinements together with transformation and upgrade of economic        性，更依托政策傳導釋放蓬勃活力。
structure laid a firmer foundation for the capital market, enabling
it to demonstrate strong resilience against volatilities and unleash
robust vitality under policy transmission.

As a “next-generation stock investing assistant”, we adhere to          作為「新一代股票投資助手」，我們堅守理性
the concepts of rational investing, value investing and long-term       投資、價值投資和長期投資理念，與投資者
investing. We advance side by side with investors, go through           並肩前行，共同經歷市場週期，共享中國經
market cycles together, and share the benefits of China’s economic      濟增長紅利。過去一年，我們以戰略定力構
growth with them. Over the past year, with our strategic focus,         建起多維產品體系，推進「科技+投研」雙輪
we developed a multi-dimensional product structure, promoted            驅動戰略，將AI深度融入產品及服務生態，
the implementation of our “technology + investment research”            只為客戶提供更簡單、更專業、更幸福的投
dual-driver strategy and deeply integrated AI technology into the       資理財服務。
ecosystem of our products and services, solely for the aim of
providing customers with investment and wealth management
services that are simpler, more professional and happier.

Due to the strategic planning precisely made by the Company,            得益於公司的精準戰略佈局，以及全體員工
and thanks to the relentless efforts of all our employees and our       和管理團隊的拼搏奮進，我們在挑戰中實現
management team, we achieved high-quality growth, steadily              高質量增長，經營效率穩步提升，業績收入
improved operational efficiency and significant revenue uplift albeit   顯著上揚。我們將持續聚焦產品力和運營效
challenges. We will continue to focus on simultaneously enhancing       率雙提升，憑藉對資本市場及個人投資者的
our product strengths and operational efficiency. Leveraging on         深刻洞察，錨定投研深度研究和應用，矢志
our profound understanding of the capital market and individual         成為「客戶投資理財的終身伴侶」。
investors and anchoring ourselves to in-depth study and application
of investment research, we are committed to becoming “a lifelong
partner in investment and wealth management for our customers”.

REVIEW OF YEAR 2024                                                     2024年度回顧
Developing a multi-dimensional product structure for the                構建多維產品體系，推動收入多元化
promotion of revenue diversification
Embracing a customer-centric philosophy, we developed a                 秉持以客戶為中心的理念，我們構建起覆蓋
full life-cycle multi-dimensional product structure. We actively        全生命週期的多維產品體系。通過深度整
diversified our revenue structure through deep integration and          合升級與創新研發，積極推動收入結構多元
upgrading and innovative R&D activities. Specifically, we integrated    化發展：對大額軟件產品整合，集中輸出專
our large-amount software products to centralize the delivery of        業投顧能力；行業首推股票學習機，填補市
our expertise in investment advisory services. We launched the          場空白，推進投資者教育；豐富小額系列產
industry-pioneering Stock Learning Machine to fill the market gap       品，匹配客戶多元需求，深度挖掘長尾市場
and promote investor education. We enriched our product offerings       潛力；加速推進App平台化轉型，不斷打磨
in the small-amount series to meet the diverse customer needs and       產品力與運營力，全方位提升用戶體驗。
deeply explore the potential of long tail markets. We stepped up in
accelerating the platform-based transformation of Apps to further
sharpen our product strengths and operational capabilities and
comprehensively enhance users’ experience.

Currently, we have formed four main product lines, namely “Stock        目前，我們已形成「股道領航系列和超級投
Navigator Series and Super Investor”, “Enjoy-Stock Pad”, “Jiuyao        資家」 「易知股道」
                                                                                 「九爻股」，以及「九方智投
Stocks” and “SmartInvest App” for the creation of synergy effects       App」四大產品線，旨在通過產品間的協同效
among products that enable us to serve a broader customer base          應，更高效服務於廣泛的客戶群體。
in a more efficient manner.

Continuing to implement our “investment research +                      推進「投研+科技」雙輪驅動，持續深耕
technology” dual-driver strategy and extensively develop                流量運營
our traffic operations
As the core corporate strategy, we firmly implemented our               作為公司核心戰略，我們堅定推進「投研+科
“investment research + technology” dual-driver approach and             技」雙輪驅動，持續深耕精細化流量運營，在
continued to extensively develop our refined traffic operations.        夯實自身專業投研能力的同時，將創新和技
While consolidating our own expertise in investment research,           術研發視為公司核心驅動力，加速推進「數
having regarded technological innovation, research and                  智化」轉型，以搶佔「AI+投顧」領域的發展先
development as the Company’s core driving forces, we accelerated        機。
the development of “digital intelligence” for seizing the first-mover
advantages in the “AI + investment advisory” sector.

Strengthening our “1+N” investment research system                      強化「1+N」投研體系，堅持買方投顧
and persisting in buyer-side investment advisory service
We strengthened our “1 research institute and N business lines”         我們強化「1 個研究所，N條業務線」投研體
investment research system, under which JF Financial Research           系－以九方金融研究所為中樞，覆蓋N條業
Institute became the hub covering N business lines and the whole        務線及全流程環節，確保研究產出實時響應
process. This ensured our research outcomes can respond to              客戶需求與市場變化，並直接落地於產品，
customer needs and market changes in real time, directly apply to       賦能業務開展。我們堅持貫徹「買方投顧」理
products and empower business operations. Consistently focusing         念，強化投研內容的實際應用，持續強化投
on the “buyer-side investment advisory” concept, we boosted             研能力建設，夯實投研實力，提升客戶投資
the practical application of our investment research outcomes,          體驗和獲得感。
continued to build up stronger investment research capabilities and
consolidated our strengths in investment research, so as to bring
an enhanced investing experience and sense of acquisition.

FINANCIAL POSITION                                                       財務狀況
Capital Structure of the Group                                           集團資本結構
By considering the cost of capital and the risks associated with         通過考慮資本成本和與每類資本相關的風
each class of capital, the Group manages its capital to ensure           險，集團以確保集團內的實體能夠持續經營
that entities in the Group will be able to continue as a going           來管理其資本，同時通過優化債務與股權平

The Board strikes a balance between the diversity of skills,                 董事會在技能、經驗和觀點多元化之間取得
experience and perspectives to meet the requirements of the                  平衡，以符合本公司業務的要求，並定期審
Company’s business, and regularly reviews the contributions                  查董事為履行對本公司的責任所需作出的貢

Duties, Responsibilities and Contributions of the Board                董事會及高級管理層的職責、責任及貢
and Senior Management                                                  獻
The Board is the core function of the Company’s corporate              董事會乃本公司企業管治架構的核心部分。
governance structure. The principal responsibilities of the Board      董事會的主要責任為設立企業管治之整體框
are to set an overall framework of corporate governance within         架及監察本公司之運營，高級管理層在企業
which senior management conducts business and to monitor               管治之整體框架內開展業務。本公司已經制
the Company’s operations. The Company has formulated overall           定企業管治的整體框架，包含內部指引、內
framework of corporate governance, containing internal guidelines,     部控制政策及程序。
internal control policies and procedures.

The Board has formulated a clear policy that stipulates the            董事會已制定清晰政策，規定高級管理層應
circumstances under which senior management should report              向董事會匯報的情形，以及在代表本公司
to the Board and obtain approval from the Board before making          作出任何決定或任何承諾前應獲得董事會批
any decisions or entering into any commitments on behalf of the        准。董事會定期檢討企業管治常規，並於適
Company. The Board regularly reviews corporate governance              當時更新。
practices and updates them as and when appropriate.

The Board, through its committees, directly and indirectly leads and   董事會透過其委員會直接及間接領導及指導
guides the management, formulates strategies and oversees their        管理層，制定策略並監督其實施，監督本集
implementation, monitors the Group’s operational and financial         團的運營及財務表現，並確保建立健全的內
performance, and ensures that sound internal control and risk          部控制及風險管理系統。
management systems are in place.

All Directors, including non-executive Directors and independent       所有董事，包括非執行董事及獨立非執行董

business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the              知識及專業精神，使其能夠高效及有效地運
Board for its efficient and effective operations. The independent      作。獨立非執行董事負責確保本公司有高標
non-executive Directors are responsible for ensuring a high            準的監管報告，並在董事會對公司舉措及運
standard of regulatory reporting of the Company and playing a          營作出有效的獨立判斷時發揮平衡作用。
balanced role for the Board in exercising effective independent
judgment on the Company’s initiatives and operations.

All Directors have full and timely access to all information of the    所有董事均充分及時獲得本公司的所有資
Company and may, upon request, seek independent professional           訊，並在適當的情況下應要求尋求獨立的專
advice in appropriate circumstances at the Company’s expenses          業意見，費用由本公司承擔，以履行其對本
for discharging their duties to the Company. Upon reviewing            公司的職責。經檢討上述措施的實施及成效

is satisfied that such mechanism can ensure the availability of        獨立觀點及意見。

The Company recognizes the benefits of having a diversified               本公司認同擁有多元化董事會成員的裨益，

strategic objectives and sustainable development. The Company             公司為尋求達致董事會成員多元化會考慮多
seeks to achieve diversity of our Board through the consideration         個因素，包括但不限於專業經驗、才能、技
of a number of factors, including but not limited to professional         能、知識、文化及教育背景、性別、年齡、
experience, talents, skills, knowledge, cultural and educational          種族及服務年期。
background, gender, age, ethnicity and length of service.

the fact that there are both female and male Directors ranging            均有，董事組合均衡且具備不同的行業和部
from 38 years old to 61 years old, and that our Directors have a          門經驗，包括整體管理、信息技術、業務發

including overall management, information technology, business            們的董事會有良好的實踐。
development and finance experiences.

balance of diversified perspectives that are relevant to the              多元化視角的適當平衡，我們擬在招聘中高
Company’s business growth, we intend to promote gender diversity          層員工時促進性別多元化，以培養董事會的

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                            風險管理及內部控制
CONTROL
The Board recognizes its responsibility for risk management             董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制制度及
and internal control systems and reviews their effectiveness.           審查其有效性的責任。該等制度旨在管理而
These systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate,            不是消除無法實現業務目標的風險，並只能
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives and can only         提供合理而非絕對的保證，以防止重大誤報
provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material        或損失。
misstatement or loss.

The Board is fully responsible for assessing and determining            董事會全面負責評估及確定其在實現本公司
the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving   策略目標過程中願意承擔的風險的性質及程
the Company’s strategic objectives and for establishing and             度，並建立及維護適當有效的風險管理及內
maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and internal      部控制機制。
control mechanisms.

and overseeing their design, implementation and monitoring of risk      等對風險管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施
management and internal control systems. The Audit Committee            及監測。審核委員會負責檢討我們的財務監
is responsible for reviewing our financial control, internal control    控、內部監控及風險管理制度。本公司設立
and risk management systems. The Company has established an             獨立的內部審核部門直接向審核委員會匯報。

SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS                                                        股東的權利
Convening Extraordinary General Meeting                                     召開股東特別大會
Pursuant to Article 58 of the Articles of Association of the Company,       根據本公司組織章程細則58條，董事會可在
the Board may convene an extraordinary general meeting at any               其認為適當的任何時候召開特別股東大會。
time as it deems appropriate. Any one or more members holding, at           任何一位或以上於遞呈要求日期持有不少於
the date of deposit of the requisition, not less than one-tenth of the      本公司繳足股本（賦有於本公司股東大會上
paid-up capital of the Company having the right of voting at general        投票權）十分之一的股東於任何時候有權透過
meetings of the Company, shall at all times have the right, by              向本公司董事會或秘書發出書面請求，要求
written requisition to the Board or the secretary of the Company, to        董事會召開股東特別大會，以處理有關請求
require an extraordinary general meeting to be called by the Board          中指明的任何事項或決議案（如有），且該大
for any transaction or resolution (if any) specified in such requisition;   會應於遞呈該請求後兩個月內舉行。若遞呈
and such meeting shall be held within two months after the deposit          後21天內，董事會未有開展召開該大會之程
of such requisition. If within 21 days of such deposit, the Board           序，則遞呈要求人士可自發以同樣方式作出
fails to proceed to convene such meeting, the requisitionist(s)             此舉，該人士因董事會未能召開會議而合理
himself/herself (themselves) may do so in the same manner, and all          產生的開支由本公司向其償付。
reasonable expenses incurred by the requisitionist(s) as a result of
the failure of the Board shall be reimbursed to the requisitionist(s)
by the Company.

The Group has established a systematic governance framework                 本集團已建立系統化的可持續發展治理體
on sustainable development, creating an ESG (environmental,                 系，形成分工明確的環境、社會及管治

shareholders, and other stakeholders. An ESG Committee has                  別與評估集團面臨的ESG風險、建立並維護
been established under the Board to identify and assess ESG risks           有效的風險管理及內部控制機制，同時定期
related to the Group, establish and maintain effective mechanisms           向董事會匯報 ESG 目標達成情況及改進建
on risk management and internal control, and regularly report               議。具體治理架構可參閱「ESG管治」章節。
to the Board on progress towards relevant ESG goals and
recommendations for improvement. For details on the governance

and governance ("ESG") report (the "Report") does not contain any       （「ESG」）報告（「本報告」）不存在任何虛假記
false representations, misleading statements or major omissions,         載、誤導性陳述或重大遺漏，並對報告內容

                 Management Details for Data at G3 and Above:
                 Access Control:
                 Set password - based access. During the use process, inquiries and changes by internal and external entities
                 require approval from relevant responsible persons. Determine access rules based on the principle of minimi-
                 zation, record and audit access situations, and clean up invalid accounts.
                 Transport Security:
                 Divide security domains. For cross - domain transmission, especially for data at G3 and above, use protocols
                 such as SSL/TLS or IPSEC to encrypt the link and use the AES256 algorithm to encrypt data. Internal
                 transmission requires approval from the compliance and security departments, and external transmission
                 requires an additional approval at the director level. Unauthorized transmission of sensitive data is prohibited.
                 Storage Protection:
                 Store data in different areas and at different levels. Files of data at G3 and above are encrypted in their entirety
                 using nationally recognized cryptographic algorithms, and keys are stored and backed up by dedicated
                 personnel.

                 G3及以上數據管理細則：
                 訪問控制：
                 設置口令訪問。使用環節，內外部主體查詢、變更需經相關負責人審批，按最小化原則確定訪問規則，記錄
                 並審計訪問情況，清理失效帳號。
                 傳輸安全：
                 劃分安全域，跨域傳輸尤其是G3及以上數據，採用SSL/TLS或IPSEC等協議加密鏈路，AES256演算法加密
                 數據。內部傳輸需合規與安全部門審批，外部傳輸增加總監級別審批，禁止私自 傳輸敏感數據。
                 存儲保護：
                 分區域、分級存儲。G3及以上數據檔使用國家認可密碼演算法整檔加密，專人保管和備份密鑰。

lifecycle of intellectual property. This system effectively safeguards   護公司核心競爭力，並為行業高質量發展貢

(TCFD) recommendations in managing climate change risks. This           (TCFD)的披露建議，開展氣候變化風險管理

strategy, risk management, and metrics & targets perspectives,          標四個方面進行管理和披露，減少氣候變化

Company.

management structure encompassing the Board, the ESG                    ESG工作小組的ESG管理架構，對包括氣候

including climate change. For details, please refer to the section      告「ESG管治」章節。
headed “ESG Governance” of this Report.

Strategy: We identify and assess climate-related risks encountered      策略：我們對公司運營中所面臨的氣候風險
in our operations, outlining potential risks, opportunities, and        進行識別和評估，梳理出可能的風險、機遇
impacts. Based on these assessment results, we formulate                和影響，根據評估結果建立對應的風險、機
corresponding risk and opportunity response plans.                      遇應對方案。

Risk Identification and Assessment                                      風險識別與評估：

We have conducted an in-depth analysis of the short-term and            我們深入剖析了環境、社會及氣候因素可能
long-term risks that may arise from environmental, social, and          引發的短期及中長期風險，力求全面而準確
climate factors, aiming to comprehensively and accurately assess        地評估這些因素對我們業務可能造成的潛在
their potential impact on our business. Since our business model        影響。鑒於我們的業務模式並不涉及任何實
does not involve the operation of any physical production facilities,   體生產設施的運營，因此我們目前並未承擔

be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on      報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                  可被視作重大。

      those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and        及獲取充分及適當的審計憑證，作為我
      appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of          們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串
      not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is        謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述或凌駕
      higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve       於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐
      collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations,       而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能
      or the override of internal control.                                 發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風

•     Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the      •   了解與審核相關的內部控制，以於有關

      (h)   Leased assets                                                      (h)   租賃資產
            At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                   於合約開始時，本集團評估合約
            the contract is, or contains, a lease. This is the case                  是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約賦
            if the contract conveys the right to control the use of                  予已識別資產在一段期間內的使
            an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for                 用控制權以換取代價，即屬此情
            consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                    況。倘客戶既有權主導已識別資
            has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset             產的使用亦有權從有關使用中獲
            and to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits                 取絕大部分經濟利益，即賦予控
            from that use.                                                           制權。


            Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                       倘合約包含租賃部分及非租賃部
            non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                        分，本集團已選擇不區分非租賃
            to separate non-lease components and accounts for                        部分，並就所有租賃將各租賃部
            each lease component and any associated non-lease                        分及任何相關非租賃部分以單一
            components as a single lease component for all leases.                   租賃部分入賬。


            At the lease commencement date, the Group recognises                     於租賃開始日期，本集團確認使
            a right-of-use asset and a lease liability, except for                   用權資產及租賃負債，惟設定
            leases that have a short lease term of 12 months or                      12個月或以下短租賃期的租賃及
            less, and leases of low-value items such as laptops and                  有關手提電腦及辦公室家具等低
            office furniture. When the Group enters into a lease in                  價值物品的租賃除外。當本集團
            respect of a low-value item, the Group decides whether                   就低價值物品訂立租賃時，本集
            to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease basis. If                    團就每份租賃決定是否進行資本
            not capitalised, the associated lease payments are                       化。如不作資本化，則相關租賃
            recognised in profit or loss on a systematic basis over                  付款有系統地於租賃期內確認於
            the lease term.                                                          損益中。


            Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability is                   倘租賃資本化，租賃負債初步按
            initially recognised at the present value of the lease                   租期內應付的租賃付款現值確
            payments payable over the lease term, discounted                         認，並使用租賃隱含的利率（或
            using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that                倘該利率無法釐定，則使用相關
            rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                      增量借款利率）貼現。初步確認
            incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition, the               後，租賃負債按攤銷成本計量，
            lease liability is measured at amortised cost and interest               利息開支則使用實際利率法確
            expense is recognised using the effective interest                       認。不取決於指數或利率的可變
            method. Variable lease payments that do not depend                       租賃付款並不計入租賃負債的計
            on an index or rate are not included in the measurement                  量，並按產生自損益中扣除。
            of the lease liability, and are charged to profit or loss as
            incurred.

2   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT AND                                           2   會計判斷及估計
    ESTIMATES
    In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,            應用本集團會計政策的過程中，管理層
    management has made the following accounting judgements:               已作出以下會計判斷：


    (a)   Recognition of income taxes and deferred tax                     (a)   確認所得稅及遞延稅項資產
          assets
          Determining income tax provision involves judgement                    釐定所得稅撥備涉及對若干交易
          on the future tax treatment of certain transactions.                   的未來稅項處理的判斷。管理層
          Management evaluates tax implications of transactions                  評估交易的稅務影響並相應劃撥
          and tax provisions are set up accordingly. The tax                     稅項撥備。有關交易的稅務處理
          treatments of such transactions are reconsidered                       會定期重新檢討，以計及所有稅
          periodically to take into account all changes in tax                   法的變動。遞延稅項資產乃就可
          legislation. Deferred tax assets are recognised in respect             抵扣暫時性差異確認。由於該等
          of deductible temporary differences. As those deferred                 遞延稅項資產只有在可能有未來
          tax assets can only be recognised to the extent that it                應課稅溢利用於抵扣可抵扣暫時
          is probable that future taxable profits will be available              性差異的情況下才能確認，因此
          against which the deductible temporary differences                     管理層需要判斷以評估未來應課

                    until the reserves reach 50% of their respective                       備達到其各自註冊資本的

                    than in liquidation and can be used to make                            時除外）及可用作彌補過往

                    converted into capital in proportion to the existing                   投資者現有股權比例轉換

                    less than 25% of the entity’s registered capital.                      25%。

22 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                                        22 財務風險管理及金融工具的
   VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                              公平值
      Exposure to credit risk, liquidity risk, interest rate risk, foreign     面對的信貸風險、流動性風險、利率風
      exchange risk and price risk arises in the normal course of the          險、外匯風險及價格風險於本集團的一
      Group’s business. The Group’s exposure to these risks and                般業務過程中出現。本集團面臨的該等
      the financial risk management policies and practice used by              風險及本集團管理該等風險所用的財務
      the Group to manage these risks are described below.                     風險管理政策及常規載列如下。

      (a)   Credit risk                                                        (a)   信貸風險
            Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will                  信貸風險指對手方未履行其合約
            default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                    責任導致本集團蒙受財務損失
            financial loss to the Group. The Group did not have                      的風險。本集團並無貿易應收款
            trade receivables. The counterparties of the Group’s                     項。本集團其他應收款項的對手
            other receivables are mainly the Group’s related parties,                方主要為本集團的關聯方，管理
            management makes periodic assessments on the                             層根據歷史結算記錄及過往經驗
            recoverability of other receivables based on historical                  對其他應收款項的可收回性進行
            settlement records and past experience to accrue                         定期評估，以計提減值虧損（見
            impairment loss (see note 15(ii)). The exposure to credit                附註15(ii)）。面對的現金及現金
            risk arising from cash and cash equivalents is limited                   等價物所產生信貸風險有限，因
            because the counterparties are banks and financial                       為對手方為擁有低信貸風險的銀
            institutions with a low credit risk.                                     行及金融機構。

            The Group does not provide any guarantees which                          本集團沒有提供任何會使本集團
            would expose the Group to credit risk.                                   面臨信貸風險的擔保。

      (b)   Liquidity risk                                                     (b)   流動性風險
            Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will not be able               流動性風險指本集團將不能履行
            to meet its financial obligations as they fall due. The                  其到期財務責任的風險。本集團
            Group’s approach to managing liquidity is to ensure, as                  管理流動性的方針，是盡可能確
            far as possible, that it will have sufficient cash to meet               保其在正常或受壓情況下備有充
            its liabilities when they are due, under both normal and                 足現金，以償還到期負債，而不
            stressed conditions, without incurring unacceptable                      會產生過高損失或引致本集團聲
            losses or risking damage to the Group’s reputation.                      譽受損。

            The Group’s policy is to regularly monitor current and                   本集團的政策是定期監察現時及
            expected liquidity requirements, and to ensure that it                   預期的流動資金需求，並確保其
            maintains sufficient reserves of cash to meet its liquidity              備有充足現金儲備以滿足其短期
            requirements in the short and longer term.                               及較長期的流動資金所需。

            The following tables show the remaining contractual                      下表列示於2023 年及2024 年年
            maturities at the end of 2023 and 2024 of the Group’s                    末按合約未貼現現金流量（包括
            financial liabilities, which are based on contractual                    按約定利率或在浮息情況下按報
            undiscounted cash flows (including interest payments                     告期末當時利率計算的利息款
            computed using contractual rates or, if floating, based                  項）計算本集團金融負債的合約
            on rates current at the end of the reporting period) and                 到期日剩餘期間以及本集團或須
            the earliest date the Group can be required to pay.                      付款的最早日期。
